Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on fermi-dirac structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 769. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 911 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 769 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the fermi-dirac structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 911. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
